# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-17)

## Corpus Check
- 3587 files · ~9,737,251 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 49734 nodes · 130200 edges · 1485 communities (1231 shown, 254 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2857 edges (avg confidence: 0.68)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `980e40c1`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
